342 | class _CrispServer:
343 |     """crispasr.exe `--server` 常驻进程的托管（实时分段专用）。
344 | 
345 |     为什么需要：录音页每说完一句就切一段上传，旧路径每段 spawn 一次
346 |     crispasr.exe、模型每次重新上显存。实测（GTX 1660 SUPER / 核心 0.8.54）
347 |     固定开销 whisper base ≈0.6s、qwen3 Q8 ≈2.35s，占出字延迟 ~90%；
348 |     `--server` 只在启动时加载一次模型，POST /v1/audio/transcriptions 复用，
349 |     单段回落到 ~0.2s（实测 0.17–0.23s）。
350 | 
351 |     生命周期归引擎：录音开始按需起，录音结束宽限若干秒再停（收尾段还要
352 |     上传），换模型／批量转录前立即停——否则常驻模型与批量转录的一次性进程
353 |     会各占一份显存（4GB 卡直接 OOM）。
354 |     """
355 | 
356 |     def __init__(self, argv: list[str], cwd: str | Path):
357 |         self._argv = list(argv)
358 |         self._cwd = str(cwd)
359 |         # RLock：start() 持锁等 /health，失败路径要调 stop()（同一把锁）——
360 |         # 用普通 Lock 会在「起不来」这条路上自锁死，恰好让退路永远走不到。
361 |         self._lock = threading.RLock()
362 |         self._proc: "subprocess.Popen[str] | None" = None
363 |         self._port = 0
364 |         self._tail: deque = deque(maxlen=80)
365 | 
366 |     # ── 状态 ──
367 |     @property
368 |     def port(self) -> int:
369 |         return self._port
370 | 
371 |     def alive(self) -> bool:
372 |         return self._proc is not None and self._proc.poll() is None
373 | 
374 |     def tail(self, n: int = 6) -> str:
375 |         return "　|　".join(list(self._tail)[-n:]) or "（无输出）"
376 | 
377 |     # ── 子进程输出泵：只留最近几行，失败时拿来报原因 ──
378 |     def _pump(self) -> None:
379 |         proc = self._proc
380 |         if proc is None or proc.stdout is None:
381 |             return
382 |         try:
383 |             for line in proc.stdout:
384 |                 t = line.rstrip()
385 |                 if t:
386 |                     self._tail.append(t)
387 |         except Exception:
388 |             pass
389 | 
390 |     # ── 启动 / 停止 ──
391 |     def start(self, timeout: float = _SERVER_START_TIMEOUT_SECS) -> None:
392 |         with self._lock:
393 |             if self.alive():
394 |                 return
395 |             port = _free_local_port()
396 |             argv = self._argv + ["--server", "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", str(port)]
397 |             self._proc = subprocess.Popen(
398 |                 argv, cwd=self._cwd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
399 |                 stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
400 |                 bufsize=1, creationflags=_CREATE_NO_WINDOW, startupinfo=_STARTUP_INFO,
401 |             )
402 |             self._port = port
403 |             threading.Thread(target=self._pump, name="crispasr-server-log",
404 |                              daemon=True).start()
405 |             try:
406 |                 self._wait_healthy(timeout)
407 |             except BaseException:
408 |                 self.stop()
409 |                 raise
410 | 
411 |     def _wait_healthy(self, timeout: float) -> None:
412 |         """轮询 /health 直到就绪；进程提前退出/超时都带上输出尾巴报错。"""
413 |         deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(timeout))
414 |         url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{self._port}/health"
415 |         while time.monotonic() < deadline:
416 |             if not self.alive():
417 |                 raise RuntimeError(
418 |                     "crispasr --server 启动即退出（这份核心可能不带 --server，"
419 |                     f"或显存不足）。最后输出：{self.tail()}")
420 |             try:
421 |                 with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=2.0) as r:
422 |                     body = json.loads(r.read().decode("utf-8", "replace"))
423 |                 if str((body or {}).get("status", "")).lower() in ("ok", "ready", "loaded"):
424 |                     return
425 |             except Exception:
426 |                 pass
427 |             time.sleep(0.2)
428 |         raise RuntimeError(
429 |             f"crispasr --server 在 {timeout:.0f} 秒内未就绪"
430 |             f"（模型可能仍在加载或卡在初始化）。最后输出：{self.tail()}")
431 | 
432 |     def transcribe_file(self, path: Path | str, fields: dict | None = None,
433 |                         timeout: float = _SERVER_REQ_TIMEOUT_SECS) -> dict:
434 |         """POST /v1/audio/transcriptions（multipart）→ 解析后的 JSON。"""
435 |         if not self.alive():
436 |             raise RuntimeError("crispasr 常驻服务已退出。")
437 |         path = Path(path)
438 |         boundary = "----sttcrisp" + uuid.uuid4().hex
439 |         chunks: list[bytes] = []
440 |         for k, v in (fields or {}).items():
441 |             if v is None or v == "":
442 |                 continue
443 |             chunks.append(f"--{boundary}\r\n".encode())
444 |             chunks.append(f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="{k}"\r\n\r\n'.encode())
445 |             chunks.append(str(v).encode("utf-8") + b"\r\n")
446 |         chunks.append(f"--{boundary}\r\n".encode())
447 |         # 文件名保留原扩展名（.webm 等容器由 crispasr 内部解码器判定）
448 |         fname = f"seg{path.suffix or '.wav'}"
449 |         chunks.append(
450 |             f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="file"; filename="{fname}"\r\n'
451 |             .encode())
452 |         chunks.append(b"Content-Type: application/octet-stream\r\n\r\n")
453 |         chunks.append(path.read_bytes())
454 |         chunks.append(b"\r\n")
455 |         chunks.append(f"--{boundary}--\r\n".encode())
456 |         req = urllib.request.Request(
457 |             f"http://127.0.0.1:{self._port}/v1/audio/transcriptions",
458 |             data=b"".join(chunks),
459 |             headers={"Content-Type": f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}"})
460 |         try:
461 |             with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
462 |                 return json.loads(r.read().decode("utf-8", "replace"))
463 |         except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
464 |             detail = ""
465 |             try:
466 |                 detail = (e.read() or b"").decode("utf-8", "replace")[:300]
467 |             except Exception:
468 |                 pass
469 |             raise RuntimeError(
470 |                 f"crispasr 常驻服务返回 HTTP {e.code}：{detail or e.reason}") from None
471 | 
472 |     def stop(self) -> None:
473 |         with self._lock:
474 |             proc, self._proc = self._proc, None
475 |         if proc is None:
476 |             return
477 |         try:
478 |             if proc.poll() is None:
479 |                 proc.terminate()
480 |                 try:
481 |                     proc.wait(timeout=_SERVER_STOP_GRACE_SECS)
482 |                 except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
483 |                     proc.kill()
484 |                     proc.wait(timeout=5)
485 |         except Exception:
486 |             pass
487 |

550 |     # ══ 加载 ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
551 | 
552 |     def load(self, model_path: Path = None, crispasr_dir: Path = None,
553 |              device_id: int = 0, cb=None, aligner_path: Path = None,
554 |              backend: str | None = None, gpu_backend: str | None = "vulkan",
555 |              cpu_threads: int = 0):
556 |         """验证 crispasr.exe 与模型存在即可（子程序模式无常驻加载）。
557 | 
558 |         aligner_path：qwen3 ForcedAligner GGUF；None 时自动在模型目录（ov_models）
559 |         与 crispasr_dir 内查找（模型目录优先）。
560 |         找到 → 启用 FA（process_file 加 `-am <gguf> -falign`）；找不到 → 退回
561 |         whisper native 时间轴。
562 | 
563 |         backend：crispasr 后端名（whisper / qwen3 / qwen3-1.7b …）。None 时依
564 |         模型文件名自动推断（whisper GGML→whisper、qwen3*.gguf→qwen3-1.7b）。
565 | 
566 |         gpu_backend：crispasr.exe 的 `--gpu-backend`（vulkan / cuda）。传 None
567 |         代表这份核心是 CPU 版或使用者选了纯 CPU 推理 → 改挂 `-ng`。调用端应
568 |         依「实际安装的加速版本」给值（downloader.crispasr_gpu_backend）。
569 | 
570 |         cpu_threads：crispasr 的 `-t`；0＝自动（_default_threads），>0＝指定。
571 |         与 OpenVINO 引擎共用 settings 的 cpu_threads 键。
572 |         """
573 |         def _s(msg):
574 |             if cb:
575 |                 cb(msg)
576 | 
577 |         crispasr_dir = Path(crispasr_dir) if crispasr_dir else _DEFAULT_CRISPASR_DIR
578 |         _s("查找 CrispASR 可执行文件…")
579 |         exe = _find_exe(crispasr_dir)
580 |         if exe is None:
581 |             raise FileNotFoundError(
582 |                 f"找不到 crispasr.exe（已搜索 {crispasr_dir}）。\n"
583 |                 "请将 CrispASR Vulkan 版解压到 crispasr/ 目录。"
584 |             )
585 |         self._exe = exe
586 | 
587 |         model_path = Path(model_path) if model_path else (_DEFAULT_CRISPASR_DIR / _DEFAULT_MODEL_NAME)
588 |         if not model_path.exists():
589 |             raise FileNotFoundError(
590 |                 f"找不到 Whisper 模型：{model_path}\n"
591 |                 f"请放入 {_DEFAULT_MODEL_NAME}（OpenAI Whisper Base GGML）。"
592 |             )
593 |         self._model_path = model_path
594 |         self._device_id  = int(device_id)
595 |         self._gpu_backend = gpu_backend or None
596 |         self._threads    = int(cpu_threads) if cpu_threads and cpu_threads > 0 \
597 |             else _default_threads()

603 |         #   就整条 FA 关掉，避免对齐器吐出私用区乱码并把时间轴对歪。

608 |         ap = (Path(aligner_path) if aligner_path

610 |         if ap and ap.exists() and not self._fa_blocked:

613 |             self.use_aligner = True      # 默认开（缺文件则维持 False，退回 native）
614 |             self.aligner   = True
615 |             self._fa       = True
616 |             _s(f"FA 对齐器就绪（{ap.name}）")
617 |         else:
618 |             self._aligner_path = None
619 |             self._fa_bin   = None
620 |             self.use_aligner = False
621 |             self.aligner   = False
622 |             self._fa       = False

626 |         _gpu_tag = (self._gpu_backend or "cpu").upper()

629 |     # ══ 实时分段：常驻 --server（免每段重新加载模型）════════════════════
630 | 
631 |     def _server_argv(self) -> list[str]:
632 |         """常驻 server 的启动参数（模型/后端/设备/调优与 _build_cmd 同口径）。
633 | 
634 |         `--server` 后的 --host/--port 由 _CrispServer.start() 追加（端口动态取，
635 |         避免与其它实例/端口占用冲突）。-fa/-nf/-bo 1 是启动期解码默认值，
636 |         与文件转录那条命令保持同一套速度/质量取舍。
637 |         """
638 |         cmd = [str(self._exe), "-m", str(self._model_path)]
639 |         if self._backend and self._backend != "whisper":
640 |             cmd += ["--backend", self._backend]
641 |             # qwen3 后端的 LID 模型：显式指到本地 whisper ggml，免联网下载
642 |             # （详见 _build_cmd 同一位置的说明）。
643 |             lid = _lid_model_path(self._exe.parent) if self._exe else None
644 |             if lid:
645 |                 cmd += ["--lid-model", str(lid)]
646 |         if self._gpu_backend:
647 |             cmd += ["--gpu-backend", self._gpu_backend, "-dev", str(self._device_id)]
648 |         else:
649 |             cmd += ["-ng"]
650 |         cmd += ["-t", str(self._threads), "-fa", "-nf", "-bo", "1"]
651 |         return cmd
652 | 
653 |     def _ensure_server(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> "_CrispServer | None":
654 |         """取常驻 server（必要时启动）。失败→记一次并永久退回 spawn，返回 None。"""
655 |         if not getattr(self, "server_mode", True) or not self._exe or not self._model_path:
656 |             return None
657 |         with self._srv_lock:
658 |             if self._srv is not None and self._srv.alive():
659 |                 return self._srv
660 |             if self._srv_failed:
661 |                 return None
662 |             srv = _CrispServer(self._server_argv(), self._exe.parent)
663 |             try:
664 |                 srv.start(timeout if timeout is not None else _SERVER_START_TIMEOUT_SECS)
665 |             except Exception as e:
666 |                 self._srv_failed = True
667 |                 _warn(f"crispasr 常驻服务启动失败，实时分段改用一次性进程：{e}")
668 |                 return None
669 |             self._srv = srv
670 |             return srv
671 | 
672 |     def prepare_realtime(self) -> None:
673 |         """录音开始：后台预热常驻 server（失败静默，分段时自然退回 spawn）。"""
674 |         if not getattr(self, "server_mode", True) or self._srv_failed:
675 |             return
676 |         threading.Thread(target=self._ensure_server, name="crispasr-server-warm",
677 |                          daemon=True).start()
678 | 
679 |     def release_realtime(self) -> None:
680 |         """停掉常驻 server（录音结束／换模型／开始批量转录前调用，给显存让位）。"""
681 |         with self._srv_lock:
682 |             srv, self._srv = getattr(self, "_srv", None), None
683 |         if srv is not None:
684 |             srv.stop()
685 | 
686 |     def transcribe_realtime(self, audio_path, language: str | None = None,
687 |                             context: str | None = None) -> TranscribeResult:
688 |         """单段音频 → 文字（录音页实时出字；不产字幕文件、不做字级对齐）。
689 | 
690 |         ── 为什么改走常驻服务（2026-11 实测，GTX 1660 SUPER / 核心 0.8.54）──
691 |         旧路径每段 spawn 一次 crispasr.exe，模型每次都重新上显存：whisper base
692 |         固定开销 ≈0.6s、qwen3 Q8 ≈2.35s，占实时出字延迟 ~90%。`--server`
693 |         只加载一次模型，同样的 2s 音频单段实测 0.17–0.23s。
694 |         服务起不来（旧核心无 --server／显存不足）时自动退回一次性 spawn，
695 |         行为与改动前一致，不会因为常驻失败而丢字。
696 |         """
697 |         srv = self._ensure_server()
698 |         if srv is not None:
699 |             try:
700 |                 obj = srv.transcribe_file(audio_path, {
701 |                     # 语言名 → whisper 语言代码；None = 自动检测（沿用服务端设置）
702 |                     "language": _LANG_MAP.get(language) if language else None,
703 |                     "prompt": ((context or "").strip()[:500] or None),
704 |                     "response_format": "verbose_json",
705 |                 })
706 |                 segs = _realtime_segments_from_api(obj)
707 |                 return TranscribeResult(
708 |                     segments_rich=segs,
709 |                     diag=None if segs else "未识别到语音内容。")
710 |             except Exception as e:
711 |                 # 单次失败不永久禁用：停掉可惜的坏进程，本段退回 spawn，
712 |                 # 下一段再试常驻（服务器崩溃/显存回收等情况能自愈）。
713 |                 _warn(f"常驻服务转录失败，本段退回一次性进程：{e}")
714 |                 with self._srv_lock:
715 |                     bad, self._srv = self._srv, None
716 |                 if bad is not None:
717 |                     bad.stop()
718 |         text = self._realtime_spawn_text(audio_path, language, context)
719 |         segs = _realtime_segments_from_api({"text": text})
720 |         return TranscribeResult(segments_rich=segs,
721 |                                 diag=None if segs else "未识别到语音内容。")
722 | 
723 |     def _realtime_spawn_text(self, audio_path, language, context) -> str:
724 |         """一次性子进程的实时转录（常驻不可用时的后备，行为同旧实现）。"""
725 |         with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
726 |             out_base = Path(td) / "seg_out"

744 |     # ══ 子程序命令建构（★ 由你定夺：speed/quality 取舍）═════════════════

746 |     def _build_cmd(self, audio_path: Path, out_base: Path,
747 |                    language: str | None, word_level: bool = True,
748 |                    context: str | None = None) -> list[str]:
749 |         """组合 crispasr.exe 命令行参数。
750 | 
751 |         ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
752 |         ★ 学习贡献点：这里是整个引擎唯一有「设计取舍」的地方。
753 |           我们在 2026-06-17 的实测（Breeze-ASR-26 q5_0 / Vulkan / 126s 台语
754 |           新闻）数据如下，请据此决定默认参数：
755 | 
756 |             设置                 fallback  时间    RTF     质量
757 |             默认(-bo5+fallback)   129      142s   0.84x   人名最准
758 |             -bo 2(保留fallback)    50       47.5s  2.65x   质量平平

653 |     def _ensure_server(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> "_CrispServer | None":
654 |         """取常驻 server（必要时启动）。失败→记一次并永久退回 spawn，返回 None。"""
655 |         if not getattr(self, "server_mode", True) or not self._exe or not self._model_path:
656 |             return None
657 |         with self._srv_lock:
658 |             if self._srv is not None and self._srv.alive():
659 |                 return self._srv
660 |             if self._srv_failed:
661 |                 return None
662 |             srv = _CrispServer(self._server_argv(), self._exe.parent)
663 |             try:
664 |                 srv.start(timeout if timeout is not None else _SERVER_START_TIMEOUT_SECS)
665 |             except Exception as e:
666 |                 self._srv_failed = True
667 |                 _warn(f"crispasr 常驻服务启动失败，实时分段改用一次性进程：{e}")
668 |                 return None
669 |             self._srv = srv
670 |             return srv

674 |         if not getattr(self, "server_mode", True) or self._srv_failed:
675 |             return

679 |     def release_realtime(self) -> None:

686 |     def transcribe_realtime(self, audio_path, language: str | None = None,
687 |                             context: str | None = None) -> TranscribeResult:
688 |         """单段音频 → 文字（录音页实时出字；不产字幕文件、不做字级对齐）。
689 | 
690 |         ── 为什么改走常驻服务（2026-11 实测，GTX 1660 SUPER / 核心 0.8.54）──
691 |         旧路径每段 spawn 一次 crispasr.exe，模型每次都重新上显存：whisper base
692 |         固定开销 ≈0.6s、qwen3 Q8 ≈2.35s，占实时出字延迟 ~90%。`--server`
693 |         只加载一次模型，同样的 2s 音频单段实测 0.17–0.23s。
694 |         服务起不来（旧核心无 --server／显存不足）时自动退回一次性 spawn，
695 |         行为与改动前一致，不会因为常驻失败而丢字。
696 |         """
697 |         srv = self._ensure_server()
698 |         if srv is not None:
699 |             try:
700 |                 obj = srv.transcribe_file(audio_path, {
701 |                     # 语言名 → whisper 语言代码；None = 自动检测（沿用服务端设置）
702 |                     "language": _LANG_MAP.get(language) if language else None,
703 |                     "prompt": ((context or "").strip()[:500] or None),
704 |                     "response_format": "verbose_json",
705 |                 })
706 |                 segs = _realtime_segments_from_api(obj)
707 |                 return TranscribeResult(
708 |                     segments_rich=segs,
709 |                     diag=None if segs else "未识别到语音内容。")
710 |             except Exception as e:
711 |                 # 单次失败不永久禁用：停掉可惜的坏进程，本段退回 spawn，
712 |                 # 下一段再试常驻（服务器崩溃/显存回收等情况能自愈）。
713 |                 _warn(f"常驻服务转录失败，本段退回一次性进程：{e}")
714 |                 with self._srv_lock:
715 |                     bad, self._srv = self._srv, None
716 |                 if bad is not None:
717 |                     bad.stop()
718 |         text = self._realtime_spawn_text(audio_path, language, context)
719 |         segs = _realtime_segments_from_api({"text": text})
720 |         return TranscribeResult(segments_rich=segs,
721 |                                 diag=None if segs else "未识别到语音内容。")

854 |     # ══ 流式执行 crispasr.exe，解析 -pp 进度 → 驱动进度条 ═══════════════════
855 |     def _run_streaming(self, cmd: list[str], progress_cb=None) -> int:
856 |         """以 Popen 流式读 crispasr 输出，解析「progress = NN%」实时回报进度。
857 | 
858 |         crispasr（whisper 后端）一次处理整档（内部切 N 个 slice），`-pp` 会输出
859 |         「crispasr: progress = NN% (x/N slices)」。**qwen3 后端不输出 progress
860 |         行**（-pp 仅 whisper 后端实现，0.8.38/0.8.42 实测）——此前 qwen3 长档
861 |         转录进度条恒为 0%，使用者以为卡死、关窗取消（反馈实录）。故这里加
862 |         「行活跃度」回报：qwen3 路径下每收到一行输出就小幅推进模拟进度
863 |         （对数逼近 90%，永不触顶），至少让使用者看到「在动」。
864 | 
865 |         取消支持：cancel_event（threading.Event）置位时终止子进程并抛
866 |         RuntimeError("已取消")——读取循环阻塞在 proc.stdout 上时靠 cancel_watch
867 |         线程兜底杀进程，避免「点了取消却要等下一行输出才生效」。
868 | 
869 |         停滞看门狗：超过 _PROC_STALL_TIMEOUT_SECS 没有任何新输出行 → 视为
870 |         挂死（Vulkan 初始化卡死等），杀掉子进程并抛错，避免永久持有
871 |         self._lock 把整个引擎卡死。
872 |         """
873 |         import time as _time
874 |         proc = subprocess.Popen(
875 |             cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
876 |             stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
877 |             bufsize=1, creationflags=_CREATE_NO_WINDOW, startupinfo=_STARTUP_INFO,
878 |         )
879 |         pat = re.compile(r"progress\s*=\s*(\d+)\s*%")
880 |         tail: list[str] = []      # 末尾输出（崩溃原因诊断用）
881 |         cancel_event = getattr(self, "cancel_event", None)
882 |         is_qwen = (self._backend or "").startswith("qwen3")
883 |         # 统一 dispatch 的 whisper（挂 FA 时才走，见 _build_cmd）同样不出 progress
884 |         # 行 → 与 qwen3 共用行活跃度模拟进度。
885 |         whisper_fa = self.whisper_fa
886 |         # qwen3 活跃度进度：输出行数 → 模拟百分比（对数逼近 90）
887 |         sim_base = 0
888 | 
889 |         def _cancel_watch():
890 |             # proc.stdout 无输出的整段时间里，读取循环不醒来；由独立线程
891 |             # 负责在取消或停滞时杀掉子进程，让读取循环立刻收到 EOF。
892 |             while True:
893 |                 if cancel_event.wait(timeout=_PROC_STALL_TIMEOUT_SECS):
894 |                     return                      # 置位 = 用户取消 → 杀进程

30asstdeepseek-flash↑53,412 ↓1,0202026-11-22 22:41:43
思考过程 3,482 字

Now I understand fully. Let me summarize the model selection logic:

1. GUI (index.html + modelView.js): getModelOptions() → cores from _MODEL_CATALOG (model_catalog.py). Selecting a model → setModel(core, model) → writes settings: backend, crisp_model, whisper_size / crisp_qwen_quant.
2. Backend _load_crispasr(): reads settings, decides model_path: qwen3 GGUF in model_dir via _qwen_casr_dl, or whisper GGML in crispasr_dir; downloads if missing. Then finds aligner GGUF (find_aligner_gguf(model_dir, fa_quant, crispasr_dir)) with pattern qwen3-forced-aligner-0.6b-*.gguf, downloads if missing.
3. CrispWhisperEngine.load(): backend inferred from filename (_infer_backend: "qwen3" in name → qwen3-1.7b/qwen3; else whisper). FA detected, _fa_incompatible check, then _build_cmd adds -am <gguf> -falign.
4. FA docs: Qwen3-ForcedAligner can be attached to any transcription backend, including Whisper.

Now the task: add locally-chosen model feature:
Branch 1 (minimal): add "从本地选择……" option for the main model (FA always preset).
Branch 2: add a new "选择FA模型" selector default = preset, plus "从本地选择……", and rename original to "选择主模型".

  1 | /* ============================================================
  2 |    bridge.js — 统一前端桥接层 window.QwenAPI
  3 |    三版共享同一前端的关键：UI 只调用 QwenAPI.*，由本层检测并路由：
  4 |      • web  : 本机/局域网 HTTP（桌面 = 本机 server + Edge；端点 = LAN）
  5 |               方法打 /api/*，进度经 SSE /api/events 推回
  6 |      • mock : 纯展示（file:// 或无后端，回范例数据供设计预览）
  7 |    进度等长时间事件通过内建 event bus；web 模式由服务器以 SSE 推送，
  8 |    mock 模式自行模拟 —— UI 两者皆以 QwenAPI.on('progress', …) 接收。
  9 |    ============================================================ */
 10 | (function () {
 11 |   "use strict";
 12 | 
 13 |   // ── event bus ───────────────────────────────────────────
 14 |   const listeners = {};
 15 |   function on(ev, fn) { (listeners[ev] ||= new Set()).add(fn); return () => off(ev, fn); }
 16 |   function off(ev, fn) { listeners[ev]?.delete(fn); }
 17 |   function emit(ev, payload) { listeners[ev]?.forEach(fn => { try { fn(payload); } catch (e) { console.error(e); } }); }
 18 | 
 19 |   // 用户可见文案的 i18n 兜底：本文件先于 i18n.js 载入，但取文案的时机都在
 20 |   // load 之后（SSE 事件 / fetch 失败），届时 window.I18N 已就绪；无字典回退简中。
 21 |   function tr(key, fallback) { return (window.I18N && I18N.t(key)) || fallback; }
 22 | 
 23 |   let MODE = "mock";
 24 |   const KEY = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("k") || "";
 25 |   const isHttp = () => location.protocol === "http:" || location.protocol === "https:";
 26 | 
 27 |   // HTTP 时探测 /health：有后端 → web；纯静态/无 API → 降级 mock。
 28 |   async function probeHttp() {
 29 |     try {
 30 |       const ctrl = new AbortController();
 31 |       const t = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 1500);
 32 |       const r = await fetch("/health", { signal: ctrl.signal, headers: authHeaders() });
 33 |       clearTimeout(t);
 34 |       return r.ok;
 35 |     } catch { return false; }
 36 |   }
 37 | 
 38 |   const ready = new Promise(resolve => {
 39 |     async function settle() {
 40 |       MODE = (isHttp() && await probeHttp()) ? "web" : "mock";
 41 |       console.info("[QwenAPI] transport =", MODE);
 42 |       if (MODE === "web") connectSSE();
 43 |       resolve(MODE);
 44 |     }
 45 |     if (document.readyState === "complete") setTimeout(settle, 0);
 46 |     else window.addEventListener("load", () => setTimeout(settle, 0), { once: true });
 47 |   });
 48 | 
 49 |   // ── web：SSE 进度/状态流式 ──────────────────────────────
 50 |   function connectSSE() {
 51 |     try {
 52 |       const es = new EventSource("/api/events" + (KEY ? "?k=" + encodeURIComponent(KEY) : ""));
 53 |       es.onmessage = e => {
 54 |         try { const m = JSON.parse(e.data); if (m && m.event) emit(m.event, m.payload); }
 55 |         catch { /* 心跳/注释行，略过 */ }
 56 |       };
 57 |       // 断线提示：EventSource 自带自动重连（此处不自写重连逻辑）。pct<0 表示
 58 |       // 非常规进度 → UI 只显示 status 文案；恢复（onopen）发空 status 撤掉提示。
 59 |       es.onerror = () => emit("progress", { pct: -1, status: tr("sse.reconnecting", "连接断开，正在重连…") });
 60 |       es.onopen = () => emit("progress", { pct: -1, status: "" });
 61 |     } catch (e) { console.warn("[QwenAPI] SSE 无法建立", e); }
 62 |   }
 63 | 
 64 |   // ── web：fetch 辅助 ─────────────────────────────────────
 65 |   function authHeaders(extra) {
 66 |     const h = Object.assign({}, extra || {});
 67 |     if (KEY) h["Authorization"] = "Bearer " + KEY;
 68 |     return h;
 69 |   }
 70 |   function withKey(path) { return KEY ? path + (path.includes("?") ? "&" : "?") + "k=" + encodeURIComponent(KEY) : path; }
 71 | 
 72 |   // ── 请求超时：挂起的请求不再让 UI 永远等待 ───────────────
 73 |   //   默认 30s；apiGet/apiPost 可传 opts.timeoutMs 覆盖，0 = 不设超时。
 74 |   //   放行长等待：/api/transcribe（上传+转录，分钟级）、/api/browse-folder、
 75 |   //   /api/browse-file（阻塞在原生对话框，直到用户关闭）。
 76 |   //   计时只罩到响应头：本地 server 响应体小、紧跟头到达；请求结束后才
 77 |   //   触发的 abort 是无害 no-op。SSE（EventSource）不走此路径。
 78 |   const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 30000;
 79 |   // 转录（上传 + 推理）不设「固定分钟数」上限：耗时只取决于音频长度与机器速度。
 80 |   // 反馈 #4：53 分钟片子单跑约 25 分钟，同时开两个实例时被 GPU 争用拖过原先
 81 |   // 写死的 30 分钟线 → 前端 abort、后端仍在跑，界面报「请求超时」且拿不到产物。
 82 |   // 真正的控制面是 SSE 进度 + 「取消转换」按钮（服务端另有断连即取消）；
 83 |   // 这里的 6 小时只是「服务端静默死掉时 UI 别永远转圈」的兜底，正常撞不到。
 84 |   const TRANSCRIBE_TIMEOUT_MS = 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
 85 | 
 86 |   async function apiFetch(path, init, timeoutMs) {
 87 |     const ms = timeoutMs == null ? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS : timeoutMs;
 88 |     if (!(ms > 0)) return fetch(withKey(path), init);   // 0 → 不设超时
 89 |     const ctrl = new AbortController();
 90 |     const t = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), ms);
 91 |     try {
 92 |       return await fetch(withKey(path), Object.assign({}, init, { signal: ctrl.signal }));
 93 |     } catch (e) {
 94 |       if (e && (e.name === "AbortError" || e.name === "TimeoutError"))
 95 |         throw new Error(tr("net.timeout", "请求超时"));   // 由调用方现有 catch 路径显示
 96 |       throw e;
 97 |     } finally {
 98 |       clearTimeout(t);
 99 |     }
100 |   }
101 | 
102 |   async function apiGet(path, opts) {
103 |     const r = await apiFetch(path, { headers: authHeaders() }, opts && opts.timeoutMs);
104 |     if (!r.ok) throw new Error("HTTP " + r.status);
105 |     return r.json();
106 |   }
107 |   async function apiPost(path, body, opts) {

# ── 本地自选模型（CrispASR）─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 预设目录之外的 .bin/.gguf 也能用：settings 记绝对路径，加载时优先于预设。
#   crisp_local_model → 主模型（Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF，后端依文件名推断）
#   crisp_local_fa    → 时间轴对齐器（ForcedAligner GGUF；Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂
#                       任何转录后端，含 Whisper）
# 下拉里的两项特殊条目：已选的「本地模型：<文件名>」与动作项「从本地选择…」。
_LOCAL_PICK = "从本地选择…"
_LOCAL_PREFIX = "本地模型："
_LOCAL_MIN_BYTES = 1 << 20          # 1 MB 下限：拦下误选的空档/LFS pointer


def _local_model(s: dict, key: str) -> Path | None:
    """settings[key] 指向的本地模型档（存在且看起来完整才返回，否则 None）。"""
    raw = str(s.get(key) or "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return None
    p = Path(raw).expanduser()
    try:
        return p if p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES else None
    except OSError:
        return None

Now set_local_model:

    def set_local_model(self, path: str, kind: str = "model") -> dict:
        """把使用者自选的模型档记进 settings（CrispASR 专用）。

        kind="model" → 主模型（crisp_local_model；Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF）
        kind="fa"    → 时间轴对齐器（crisp_local_fa；ForcedAligner GGUF，
                       Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂在任何转录后端上，含 Whisper）
        传空路径 = 清除该自选项，回到预设。
        """
        key = {"fa": "crisp_local_fa"}.get(kind, "crisp_local_model")
        raw = str(path or "").strip().strip('"')
        if not raw:
            self.settings.update({key: ""})
            return {"ok": True, "cleared": True,
                    "message": "已改回预设模型。"}
        p = Path(raw).expanduser()
        if not p.is_file():
            return {"ok": False, "error": f"找不到文件：{p}"}
        if p.suffix.lower() not in (".bin", ".gguf"):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "请选择 .bin（Whisper GGML）或 .gguf（Qwen3-ASR / 对齐器）模型档。"}
        if p.stat().st_size < _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "该文件过小，不像是完整模型。"}
        self.settings.update({key: str(p)})
        return {"ok": True, "path": str(p), "name": p.name,
                "message": f"已选定本地{'对齐' if key.endswith('_fa') else ''}模型「{p.name}」，点「下载并加载模型」即可载入。"}

Note that settings.update doesn't change backend; if backend is openvino and user picks local model, they'd need to switch core. Frontend only shows the picker under CrispASR core?     def browse_file(self, start: str = "", title: str = "选择文件",

Note tkinter filetypes patterns can be "*.bin *.gguf" in one string? tkinter passes to Tk; multiple patterns in one string separated by spaces works on Windows ("*.bin *.gguf")? Safer: [("模型文件", "*.bin;*.gguf")] on Windows uses semicolon. Cross-platform: Tk accepts a space-separated list in the pattern string. Actually Tk's filetypes pattern is a list like {"*.txt"} and supports multiple patterns: pattern can be a Tcl list. In Python, passing "*.bin *.gguf" gets Tcl-split into a list of two patterns. Yes, that works.

function populateModels(coreLabel, modelLabel) {
  const core = coreObj(coreLabel); if (!core) return;
  const sel = $("#model-select"); sel.innerHTML = "";
  core.models.forEach(m => {
    const o = document.createElement("option");
    o.value = m.label; o.textContent = m.label; o.dataset.arch = m.arch;
    o.dataset.note = m.note || "";
    if (m.action) o.dataset.action = m.action;
    if (m.local) o.dataset.local = "1";
    if (m.label === modelLabel) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });

}

async function pickLocalModel(kind) {
  let r;
  try { r = await API.browseFile("", kind === "fa" ? "选择时间轴对齐模型（GGUF）" : "选择模型文件（.bin / .gguf）", kind); }
  catch (err) { ... }
  if (!r || !r.ok || !r.path) { await renderModel(); return; }   // 取消 → 回弹当前选择
  const res = await API.setLocalModel(r.path, kind);
  if (!res || res.ok === false) { modelMsg((res && res.error) || "套用失败", "warn"); await renderModel(); return; }
  modelMsg(res.message, "info");
  await renderModel();     // 重建下拉：出现「本地模型：xxx」并被选中
  updateLoadBtn();
}

def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
    try:
        p = Path(p)
        return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES
    except OSError:
        return False

  const sel = $("#model-select"); sel.innerHTML = "";
  core.models.forEach(m => {
    const o = document.createElement("option");
    o.value = m.label; o.textContent = m.label; o.dataset.arch = m.arch;
    o.dataset.note = m.note || "";
    if (m.label === modelLabel) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  if (!core.models.some(m => m.label === modelLabel) && core.models[0]) sel.value = core.models[0].label;
  updateArch();
}

New:

function populateModels(coreLabel, modelLabel) {
  const core = coreObj(coreLabel); if (!core) return;
  const sel = $("#model-select"); sel.innerHTML = "";
  core.models.forEach(m => {
    const o = document.createElement("option");
    o.value = m.label; o.textContent = m.label; o.dataset.arch = m.arch;
    o.dataset.note = m.note || "";
    if (m.action) o.dataset.action = m.action;   // "browse" = 动作项，不是真模型
    if (m.label === modelLabel) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  if (!core.models.some(m => m.label === modelLabel) && core.models[0]) sel.value = core.models[0].label;
  updateArch();
}

And the count in renderCoreCards:

      <div class="d">${STTUtil.escapeHtml(T("model.nModels", `${core.models.length} 种模型可选`, { n: core.models.length }))}</div>

→ count real models:

      <div class="d">${STTUtil.escapeHtml(T("model.nModels", `${_realModelCount(core)} 种模型可选`, { n: _realModelCount(core) }))}</div>

with helper:

function _realModelCount(core) {
  return (core.models || []).filter(m => !m.action).length;
}

Hmm, the local dynamic entry counts as a model; that's fine/desirable.

→

// 换模型 → 套用；选中「从本地选择…」→ 开原生文件对话框
$("#model-select").addEventListener("change", async e => {
  const opt = e.target.selectedOptions[0];
  if (opt && opt.dataset.action === "browse") { await pickLocalModel("model"); return; }
  updateArch();
  await applyModel(_curCore, e.target.value);
});

// 本地自选模型（kind: "model" 主模型 / "fa" 对齐器）：选文件 → 写 settings → 刷新下拉
async function pickLocalModel(kind) {
  const title = kind === "fa" ? T("model.pickFaTitle", "选择时间轴对齐模型（*.gguf）")
                              : T("model.pickModelTitle", "选择模型文件（*.bin / *.gguf）");
  let r = null;
  try { r = await API.browseFile("", title, kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(String(err), "warn"); }
  if (!r || !r.ok || !r.path) { await refreshModelOptions(null); return; }   // 取消 → 回弹
  let res = null;
  try { res = await API.setLocalModel(r.path, kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(T("model.applyFail", "套用失败：") + (err.message || err), "warn"); }
  await refreshModelOptions(res && res.message);
  if (res && res.error) modelMsg(res.error, "warn");
}

// 重新拉取目录与当前选择并重建下拉（不重画设备/自检，避免闪动）
async function refreshModelOptions(message) {
  try {
    _modelOpt = await API.getModelOptions();
    _curCore = _modelOpt.current.core;
    renderCoreCards();
    populateModels(_curCore, _modelOpt.current.model);
    if (message) modelMsg(message, "info");
    updateLoadBtn();
  } catch (e) {}
}

收到，切到 bash 了，后续命令我用 POSIX 写法。

先把「模型是怎么被选出来的」这条链路说清楚，再动手改。我已经把相关代码都读完了。

## 现在的选区逻辑（4 段链路）

① 目录（唯一的“真理源”） — model_catalog.py 的 _MODEL_CATALOG，每行是 (引擎标签, 模型标签, backend, settings patch)。GUI 的下拉列表就是它；选一项 = 把 patch 写进 settings.json（crisp_model + whisper_size／crisp_qwen_quant）。

② 点选 — webview_backend.set_model() 按标签反查目录项 → settings.update({"backend": ..., **patch})。之后 _current_selection() 把 settings *反推*成标签，用来回填下拉；_identity_for() 把 settings 压成指纹，决定“要不要重新加载”。

③ 加载 — _load_crispasr() 读 settings 决定 model_path：Qwen3 系列 → model_dir/下载器给的文件名；否则 → crispasr_dir/ggml-*.bin；缺档就自动下载。FA 对齐器是独立的一支：find_aligner_gguf() 按固定文件名 qwen3-forced-aligner-0.6b-<quant>.gguf 找（模型目录优先、crispasr 目录备援），缺则下载。

④ 引擎 — CrispWhisperEngine.load() 不看 settings，只看文件名：_infer_backend() 里 "qwen3" in name → qwen3-1.7b，否则 whisper。然后 _build_cmd() 拼 -m 模型、-am 对齐器 -falign。

关键洞察：主模型和 FA 是两条互不依赖的支路，而且模型路径只由“文件名”参与推断——这正是能塞进“本地自选”的位置。

## 方案1 的最小改动设计

只加一个 settings 键 crisp_local_model（绝对路径），并让它优先于预设；FA 完全不动。下拉里追加两项：已选中的 本地模型：<文件名>（动态项）和动作项 从本地选择…（点它开原生文件对话框，选完回调写入 settings）。共 5 个文件、无新依赖。

+# ── 本地自选模型（CrispASR）──────────────────────────────────────────────
+# 预设目录之外的 .bin/.gguf 也能用：settings 记绝对路径，加载时优先于预设。
+#   crisp_local_model → 主模型（Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF；后端由
+#                       crisp_engine._infer_backend 依文件名推断）
+#   crisp_local_fa    → 时间轴对齐器（ForcedAligner GGUF）。官方文档：
+#                       Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂在任何转录后端上，含 Whisper。
+# 下拉里的两个特殊条目：已选中的「本地模型：<文件名>」与动作项「从本地选择…」。
+_LOCAL_PICK = "从本地选择…"
+_LOCAL_PREFIX = "本地模型："
+_LOCAL_MIN_BYTES = 1 << 20      # 1 MB 下限：拦下空档 / Git-LFS pointer
+
+
+def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
+    """路径指向的档存在且大小像完整模型（排除空档与 LFS pointer）。"""
+    try:
+        p = Path(p).expanduser()
+        return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES
+    except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
+        return False
+
+
+def _local_model(s: dict, key: str = "crisp_local_model") -> Path | None:
+    """settings[key] 指向的本地模型档；未设／失效一律 None（调用端回退预设）。"""
+    raw = str((s or {}).get(key) or "").strip()
+    if not raw:
+        return None
+    p = Path(raw).expanduser()
+    return p if _local_file_ok(p) else None
+

So when I edit webview_server.py and i18n.js I must preserve CRLF, otherwise the whole file shows as rewritten in the diff... Actually no: if I insert lines with LF into a CRLF file, git diff will show only those lines changed but with a "mixed line endings" artifact — the diff could include the whole line ending mismatch? Git diff compares line content including CR. Inserting LF-only lines into a CRLF file: only the added lines differ (their content lacks CR), so the diff shows only the added lines — plus possibly ^M noise. It doesn't rewrite other lines. But the file becomes mixed EOL, which is bad practice. Better: preserve CRLF in those files.

My editor writes new_text as given — with \n. So new lines would be LF. Hmm. Can I include \r in new_text? I can type \r literally? The JSON string could contain "\r\n" escapes. The tool takes new_text as a string; if I pass actual CR characters, they'd be written. In the JSON I can write "\r\n" escape sequences which become real CR LF. But the exact old_text match also needs CRLF — matching is probably exact on the file content, so old_text must include \r. I can't reliably type CR in the JSON... Actually I can: in JSON I can use "\r\n" escape. The tool receives real CRLF chars. That should work for both old_text and new_text as long as the file uses CRLF everywhere.

That's finicky and error-prone. Alternative approach for CRLF files: keep the diff minimal by making edits only in LF files, and for the CRLF files (webview_server.py route addition, i18n.js keys) use a script (python) to do the insertion preserving CRLF. Or use the editor with \r\n escapes.

Now browse_file kind param, _has_any_model local check, health_check whisper item.

browse_file current:

    def browse_file(self, start: str = "", title: str = "选择文件") -> dict:
        """弹出原生「选择文件」对话框（用于 FFmpeg 可执行文件路径）。

        返回 {"ok": bool, "path": str}；用户取消 → ok=False。
        """
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            from tkinter import filedialog
            root = tk.Tk()
            root.withdraw()
            root.attributes("-topmost", True)
            try:
                initial = Path(start) if start and Path(start).exists() else None
                path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
                    title=title, initialdir=str(initial.parent) if initial else "",
                    initialfile=initial.name if initial else "",
                    filetypes=[("可执行文件", "*.exe"), ("所有文件", "*.*")],
                    parent=root)
            finally:
                root.destroy()
            return {"ok": bool(path), "path": path or ""}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"ok": False, "path": "", "error": str(e)}

Change signature + docstring + filetypes.

Contrast with _current_selection / selected_model_present where I intentionally used the raw setting string: display keeps showing 本地模型：x even if the file is gone, and present returns False. That's the deliberate asymmetry: raw = "user's intent", _local_model = "usable".

Answer:

- _local_model() 返回的是“这个路径现在还能不能用”，校验 is_file() + ≥1MB；文件消失 → None → is not None 为 False。
- 在 _has_any_model 里，这个 False 不会直接 return False，而是继续往下查其它模型来源（预设 whisper/qwen/OV）。所以“本地模型消失”只意味着这一项不算数，不会误报 True，也不会因此把“已有模型”判成 False（如果机器上还有别的模型）。
- 另一处我有意区分：_current_selection() / selected_model_present() 用的是原始 settings 字符串（raw），所以文件被删后下拉仍显示「本地模型：x」，而 selected_model_present() 返回 False → 前端显示“未下载 + 检查明细里是那条绝对路径”。这是“意图 vs 可用性”的两层：raw 表示用户选了它，_local_model 表示它可用。

Then run a compile check + diff review.

修好了。同时回答你的问题：不会 return True。

关键在 _local_model() 的语义——它不是"读设置"，而是"这个档现在还能不能用"：

def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
    return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES   # 消失/空档 → False

所以本地档被删后 _local_model(...) 回 None，is not None 为 False，_has_any_model 只是跳过这一项继续往下查（预设 whisper / qwen / OV）。它不会因为本地档没了就误报"已有模型"，但也不会因此把整机判成"没有模型"——如果 ov_models 里还有预设档，那仍然该是 True。

这里我刻意做了「意图 vs 可用性」两层，别混用：

| 场景 | 用原始 settings 字符串 | 用 _local_model() |
|---|---|---|
| _current_selection 下拉回显 | ✅ 文件删了仍显示「本地模型：x」 | ❌ 会悄悄跳回预设，用户看不出自己的选择没了 |
| selected_model_present | — | ✅ 回 False → 前端走"未下载"+检查明细 |
| _has_any_model | — | ✅ 只有真能加载才算"已有模型" |

也就是说：文件消失时界面会停在「本地模型：x 未就绪」并给出绝对路径，而不是静默换成别的模型去跑。

Let me write a script:

python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path("webview_server.py")
b = p.read_bytes()
old = (b'                    if path == "/api/model":\r\n'
       b'                        body = self._read_json_body()\r\n'
       b'                        return self._json(server.backend.set_model(\r\n'
       b'                            body.get("core"), body.get("model")))\r\n')
new = old + (b'                    if path == "/api/set-local-model":   # 本地自选模型（主模型／对齐器）\r\n'
             b'                        body = self._read_json_body()\r\n'
             b'                        return self._json(server.backend.set_local_model(\r\n'
             b'                            body.get("path"), body.get("kind", "model")))\r\n')
assert b.count(old) == 1, b.count(old)
b = b.replace(old, new)

old2 = (b'                        return self._json(server.backend.browse_file(\r\n'
        b'                            body.get("start", ""), body.get("title", "选择文件")))\r\n')
new2 = (b'                        return self._json(server.backend.browse_file(\r\n'
        b'                            body.get("start", ""), body.get("title", "选择文件"),\r\n'
        b'                            body.get("kind", "exe")))\r\n')
assert b.count(old2) == 1, b.count(old2)
b = b.replace(old2, new2)
p.write_bytes(b)
print("ok")
PY

144asstdeepseek-flash↑145,206 ↓1,0022026-11-22 23:15:31
思考过程 2,233 字

The user asks: since there's a separate local model mechanism, why did I change browseFile?

Answer: because the new "从本地选择…" flow needs the native file dialog, and the existing browse_file backend had a hard-coded filter [("可执行文件","*.exe")] — designed for picking ffmpeg.exe. If I reuse it as-is, the user picks a .bin/.gguf and the dialog only shows .exe files (on Windows, the filter hides everything else unless you switch to "所有文件"). So the kind parameter is the minimal way to reuse the existing dialog instead of writing a second one.

Alternatives:
1. Leave browseFile untouched and write a dedicated /api/browse-model + browse_model() function → more code (new endpoint + new backend method) duplicating ~15 lines of tkinter boilerplate.
2. Make browseFile take an optional filter (what I did) → 3 files touched slightly, zero duplication.
3. Pass nothing and let users switch to "所有文件" manually → zero code, but a dialog that appears to "not show my model" is a real usability trap.

Also note: title too — otherwise the dialog says "选择文件" and users can't tell whether they're picking the main model or the FA. That's the other param.

But — is it really necessary? Let me reconsider: the kind default is "exe" so the ffmpeg call site is unchanged (that's why I kept a default). The addition is backward-compatible.

I should also point out the alternative if they prefer an even smaller diff: keep browseFile(start) and only show "所有文件". I'd argue the filter matters.

Let me also double-check the edit is minimal & backward compatible: yes, browseFile(start) still works; server passes kind default "exe"; backend defaults "exe".

Keep the answer short, 1-2 paragraphs, with a table or bullet. And then ask if they'd rather drop it (one question) — actually I can just explain and offer to revert if they want the absolute minimal diff. Since they're doing a PR, they may prefer to avoid touching the ffmpeg path at all.

I'll explain and ask ONE question: keep the shared kind param vs. a separate /api/browse-model endpoint (or drop filter entirely).

Actually the teacher contract: end turn when the student needs to choose. Good — ask one question with clear options.

Let me not call tools now.

因为「从本地选择…」需要一个原生文件对话框，而仓库里现成的只有一个 browseFile，它的后端写死了只显示可执行文件：

filetypes=[("可执行文件", "*.exe"), ("所有文件", "*.*")]

复用它但什么都不改，用户点「从本地选择…」会看到一个只列 .exe 的窗口——模型文件被过滤器藏起来了，这属于"看起来没反应"的坑。

于是我给它加了两个带默认值的可选参数，调用点全部向后兼容：

| 参数 | 不传时 | 用途 |
|---|---|---|
| kind | "exe" | model → *.bin *.gguf；fa → *.gguf；ffmpeg 那条老路径完全不变 |
| title | "选择文件" | 区分"选主模型"还是"选对齐器"，否则两个对话框长得一模一样 |

三个备选方案，代价不同：

1. 共享 kind（现在的做法）：改 3 个文件各几行，零重复代码，老调用点行为不变。
2. 另开 /api/browse-model + browse_model()：完全不动 ffmpeg 那条链，但要复制 ~15 行 tkinter 样板。
3. 两个参数都不加：零改动，但用户得自己切到"所有文件"才能看到模型。

你选哪个？

Current:

// 换模型 → 套用
$("#model-select").addEventListener("change", async e => {
  updateArch();
  await applyModel(_curCore, e.target.value);
});

New:

// 换模型 → 套用；选中「从本地选择…」则先开原生对话框（动作项，不套用）
$("#model-select").addEventListener("change", async e => {
  const opt = e.target.selectedOptions[0];
  if (opt && opt.dataset.action === "browse") { await pickLocalModel("model"); return; }
  updateArch();
  await applyModel(_curCore, e.target.value);
});

// 本地自选模型：选文件 → 写 settings → 重建下拉（kind: "model" 主模型）
async function pickLocalModel(kind) {
  let r = null;
  try { r = await API.browseFile("", T("model.pickLocalTitle", "选择本地模型文件"), kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(String(err), "warn"); }
  if (!r || !r.ok || !r.path) { await refreshModelOptions(); return; }  // 取消 → 回弹
  let res = null;
  try { res = await API.setLocalModel(r.path, kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(T("model.applyFail", "套用失败：") + (err.message || err), "warn"); }
  if (res && res.error) modelMsg(res.error, "warn");
  await refreshModelOptions(res && res.ok ? res.message : null);
}

// 重取目录+当前选择并重建下拉（不重画设备/自检，避免整页闪动）
async function refreshModelOptions(message) {
  try {
    _modelOpt = await API.getModelOptions();
    _curCore = _modelOpt.current.core;
    renderCoreCards();
    populateModels(_curCore, _modelOpt.current.model);
    if (message) modelMsg(message, "info");
    updateLoadBtn();
  } catch (e) {}
}

刚才那行只做了一件事：给 action 项打标记。

if (m.action) o.dataset.action = m.action;   // "browse" = 动作项（不是真模型）

下拉里现在混了两类东西：真模型（预设 10 项 + 已选中的本地档）和一个动作（从本地选择…）。<select> 只能通过 change 事件告诉你"选中了第几项"，分不出是哪一类。打了 dataset.action 之后，处理函数可以判断：

if (opt.dataset.action === "browse") { /* 开对话框 */ return; }

这样就不用在 JS 里硬比较中文字符串 "从本地选择…"——那句话只在后端定义一次。

Command:

python -c "
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('webview/js/i18n.js'); b=p.read_bytes()
old=('    \"model.pick\": [\"选择模型\", \"Choose a model\"],\r\n').encode('utf-8')
new=old+('    \"model.pickLocalTitle\": [\"选择本地模型文件（.bin / .gguf）\", \"Choose a local model file (.bin / .gguf)\"],\r\n').encode('utf-8')
assert b.count(old)==1
p.write_bytes(b.replace(old,new,1))
print('i18n patched')
"

Multi-line python -c inside double quotes in bash: \r\n inside a Python string literal — bash double quotes keep backslashes except before $, , ", \, newline. \r stays as \r` → Python sees escape → OK.

But the tool may mangle newlines in the command string (as with heredoc). The earlier failure was heredoc parsing; a python -c "..." with embedded newlines might also break. Safer: write a temp script file with the editor and run it. Let me do that (reliable).

The user asks two things:

1. When the local choice is cleared (empty), will the dropdown show "本地模型：无" or just the normal model? — Answer: in get_model_options, the dynamic local entry is only appended if raw_local: (non-empty), so an empty/cleared value means the entry disappears entirely; the dropdown falls back to preset labels, and _current_selection won't return a 本地模型 label. So no "本地模型：无" entry.

But careful: is there any path where the local file exists but the label would be missing? No.

Also selected_model_present for empty → falls to preset checks. Good.

2. Why patch = dict(patch)? Explain: patch comes from the catalog entry — an object shared with the module-level _MODEL_CATALOG list. Mutating it would permanently pollute the catalog: _MODEL_CATALOG entries' patch dicts are reused by get_model_options? No — get_model_options only uses labels/backend, not patch. But selected_model_present_for(backend, patch) in CLI uses patches from the catalog; if I had injected crisp_local_model: "" into the catalog dict, CLI profiles would silently rewrite that key. And more importantly it'd be a global side effect on a module-level constant — a real bug across calls.

So dict(patch) = copy before mutation to avoid mutating the shared catalog dict. Alternatively use {**patch, "crisp_local_model": ""} without rebinding:

cur = self.settings.update({"backend": backend, **patch,
                            **({"crisp_local_model": ""} if backend == "crispasr" else {})})

or

extra = {"crisp_local_model": ""} if backend == "crispasr" else {}
cur = self.settings.update({"backend": backend, **patch, **extra})

That avoids the reassignment (which shadows the tuple-unpacked patch and can confuse readers). The user seems to prefer the clearer form. Let me switch to the extra version — no rebinding, no mutation.

entry = next(e for e in _MODEL_CATALOG if ...)
core_label, model_label, backend, patch = entry     # patch 指向目录里的 dict

一旦原地改，_MODEL_CATALOG 从第二行起就带着这个键了，而 cli_mode 的 selected_model_present_for(backend, patch) 正是拿目录 patch 当 settings 快照用——污染会静默传给 CLI。

                            _QWEN_CASR_MODELS, _QWEN_CASR_NOTE, _qwen_casr_dl,
                            _COMMON_LANGS)
 
+# ── 本地自选模型（CrispASR）──────────────────────────────────────────────
+# 预设目录之外的 .bin/.gguf 也能用：settings 记绝对路径，加载时优先于预设。
+#   crisp_local_model → 主模型（Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF；后端由
+#                       crisp_engine._infer_backend 依文件名推断）
+#   crisp_local_fa    → 时间轴对齐器（ForcedAligner GGUF）。官方文档：
+#                       Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂在任何转录后端上，含 Whisper。
+# 下拉里的两个特殊条目：已选中的「本地模型：<文件名>」与动作项「从本地选择…」。
+_LOCAL_PICK = "从本地选择…"
+_LOCAL_PREFIX = "本地模型："
+_LOCAL_MIN_BYTES = 1 << 20      # 1 MB 下限：拦下空档 / Git-LFS pointer
+
+
+def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
+    """路径指向的档存在且大小像完整模型（排除空档与 LFS pointer）。"""
+    try:
+        p = Path(p).expanduser()
+        return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES
+    except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
+        return False
+
+

Hmm that's the truncation cutting mid-line. Let me check that region directly to make sure there's no stray whitespace-only line.

Also _selected_model_detail uses detail["missing"] for the local absolute path, but frontend renders it under "缺少 N 个文件：" relative to modelDir — acceptable.

One more issue: populateModels sets o.dataset.arch = m.arch — for the action item arch is "CrispASR · 本地文件", and updateArch() is skipped for action. Fine.

Another: after pickLocalModel success we call refreshModelOptions(message), which calls updateLoadBtn() → it fetches status; st.selectedReady should now be true (file exists) → the button may show "前往语音转文字" if modelReady is true and identity unchanged... Actually identity changed (local path differs) but updateLoadBtn compares st.selectionDiffers? It checks st.selectedReady and res.identityChanged. Since we pass no res, it will use st.modelReady && selectedReady && !accelNeeds && !(res && res.identityChanged) → true → button says "前往语音转文字" — wrong! User just picked a new model, needs to load it.

Look at updateLoadBtn(res, accelNeeds):

  const selectedReady = (st.selectedReady !== false) && !(st.selectedDetail && st.selectedDetail.coreIssue);
  if (st.modelReady && selectedReady && !accelNeeds && !(res && res.identityChanged)) {
    btn → goto

So for the local pick, I must pass the res (with identityChanged true) to updateLoadBtn. In refreshModelOptions I don't have res. Options:
- Pass the res through: refreshModelOptions(message, res) and call updateLoadBtn(res).

Simplest: in pickLocalModel, after refresh, call updateLoadBtn(res) if identityChanged. But refreshModelOptions already calls updateLoadBtn() with no args; call again with res. Slightly wasteful but fine. Cleaner: give refreshModelOptions a second param.

Let me restructure:

async function refreshModelOptions(message, res) {
  try {
    _modelOpt = await API.getModelOptions();
    _curCore = _modelOpt.current.core;
    renderCoreCards();
    populateModels(_curCore, _modelOpt.current.model);
    if (message) modelMsg(message, "info");
    await updateLoadBtn(res || null);

Current:

    def browse_file(self, start: str = "", title: str = "选择文件",
                    kind: str = "exe") -> dict:
        """弹出原生「选择文件」对话框（FFmpeg 可执行文件／本地模型档）。

        kind 决定文件类型过滤：exe（默认）／model（*.bin *.gguf）／fa（*.gguf）。
        返回 {"ok": bool, "path": str}；用户取消 → ok=False。
        """
        types = {
            "model": [("模型文件（*.bin *.gguf）", "*.bin *.gguf"),
                      ("所有文件", "*.*")],
            "fa":    [("对齐器 GGUF（*.gguf）", "*.gguf"), ("所有文件", "*.*")],
        }.get(kind) or [("可执行文件", "*.exe"), ("所有文件", "*.*")]

→ remove fa line, docstring says exe/model.

// kind="model"（主模型）／"fa"（时间轴对齐器，方案2 用同一入口）
async function pickLocalModel(kind) {

注：没打算测试。
……等一下，CrispASR不是把所有模型一视同仁的吗？？？
确认一下其实现。
附另一AI的回复：
---
这段来自“某AI”的言论，如果属实，那简直是精准踩中了软件工程里的经典反模式（Anti-pattern）。必须得狠狠地批判一下，尤其是考虑到你正准备开 PR 做“自定义模型路径”功能！

以下是对这段言论的深度批判与拆解：

### 🚨 批判点一：靠“文件名”猜类型，极其脆弱的“屎山”逻辑
_infer_backend() 靠检查文件名里有没有 qwen3 来推断后端架构，这种做法在技术上极其荒谬。
* 文件名是用户资产，不是程序状态。用户下载模型后，为了管理方便，极大概率会把长长的名字改成 qwen.gguf、model_q8.gguf 甚至 测试用.gguf。
* GGUF 标准形同虚设：GGUF 格式最大的优势之一，就是它的头部（Header）包含了丰富的元数据（Metadata），其中就包括 general.architecture（架构名称，如 qwen2、whisper 等）。放着好好的内部元数据不读，去匹配文件名，这纯粹是为了赶进度写的“偷懒代码”。

### 🛑 批判点二：甩锅给“报错”，掩盖体验的灾难
那个 AI 说：“报错而不是静默跑错，但体验上不友好”。
* 这根本不是体验好不好的问题，这是 Bug。用户传入了一个合法的、经过转换的 Qwen GGUF，只因为改了名字，程序就判断它是 Whisper，然后强行用 Whisper 的算子去跑，最后因为维度不匹配崩溃报错。这不叫“有兜底”，这叫“由于前端的愚蠢，导致后端的无辜崩溃”。

### 🎯 批判点三：职责错位（前端造轮子，后端背黑锅）
STTMiniTool 作为一个套壳前端，它绝对不应该自己去“猜”模型是什么架构。
* CrispASR 本身就是通才。底层引擎已经内置了强大的自动检测能力（通过 GGUF 的元数据），只要你把路径传给它（-m path/to/model.gguf），它自己就知道该用哪套推理逻辑。
* 这个 _infer_backend() 的存在，说明作者在封装底层 CLI 时，为了在 GUI 上动态显示“当前使用的是 Qwen 还是 Whisper”，搞了个硬编码的字符串匹配。

---

### 🛠️ 对你 PR 的直接启发：怎么绕开这个坑？

既然你打算做“自定义模型路径”，这段“某AI”的情报对你极其宝贵，它直接指明了你要修改的靶点。

你的 PR 方案可以这样写（让作者无法拒绝）：

1. 废除或重构 _infer_backend()：
不要再读文件名了！如果非要在界面上显示当前用的是什么模型，去读 GGUF 的 Header 信息。Python 下用 gguf 这个包，两行代码就能读出 general.architecture。

    from gguf import GGUFReader
    reader = GGUFReader("model.gguf")
    arch = reader.get_field("general.architecture") # 会返回 'qwen2' 或 'whisper'

2. 把“猜测”的权利交还给用户（Fallback）：
如果读取元数据失败，在 UI 上加一个下拉框：“强制指定后端架构”（选项：自动检测 / Qwen3-ASR / Whisper / Voxtral）。用户选什么，前端就传什么给 CrispASR 的 --backend 参数。
3. 最简单的解法：直接不传 --backend：
你在 PR 里可以告诉作者：“既然我们是把绝对路径传给底层 crispasr.exe，我们完全可以不传 --backend 参数，让 CrispASR 自己去读元数据自动识别。这样前端代码最干净，且兼容未来所有新出的模型。”

### 💡 总结
那个 AI 的情报很准，不仅点出了一个隐蔽的 Bug，还为你指明了 PR 的切入点。你可以借着修复这个 Bug 的名义，把“自定义模型路径”的功能做得极其优雅。

在 PR 描述里，你可以直接对作者说：
> “Hi, while implementing the custom model path feature, I noticed _infer_backend() relies on filename string matching. This causes issues if users rename their .gguf files. I've refactored it to either parse the GGUF header for general.architecture or simply let the CrispASR CLI auto-detect it. This makes custom model loading seamless.”

这不是 Vibe 了，这是直接帮他修底层的技术债！去干吧！

        你只需把速度/质量相关标志填入 `tuning`（约 1–4 个元素）。
        ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        """
        cmd = [
            str(self._exe),
            "-m", str(self._model_path),
        ]
        # 非 whisper 架构（Qwen3-ASR 等）需显式指定后端，crispasr 才用对解码路径。
        # whisper 平时**不传**：省略 --backend 时 crispasr 走 legacy whisper 主路径，
        # 功能最全且 -pp 有真实进度行。但该路径没接线 CTC 对齐器——-am/-falign 被
        # 静默忽略（0.8.44/0.8.48/0.8.49 实测输出逐字节相同）；因此仅在挂 FA 时才
        # 显式 --backend whisper 路由进统一 dispatch，对齐器才会生效（代价：统一
        # 路径不出 progress 行，进度改由 _run_streaming 走行活跃度模拟，与 qwen3
        # 同款；实测 0.8.44 起统一路径支持 --backend whisper）。
        unified_whisper = (self._backend == "whisper" and word_level
                           and self.use_aligner and bool(self._aligner_path))
        if self._backend:
            if self._backend != "whisper" or unified_whisper:
                cmd += ["--backend", self._backend]
            if self._backend != "whisper":
                # qwen3 后端默认要用 ggml-tiny.bin 做 LID（语言识别）；找不到时
                # crispasr 会自己联网下载，网络不通就干等 ~44 秒才继续（0.8.38/0.8.42
                # 实测）。这里显式指到本地的 whisper ggml（base 已随包），彻底免下载。
                lid = _lid_model_path(self._exe.parent) if self._exe else None
                if lid:
                    cmd += ["--lid-model", str(lid)]
        # 加速版本：Vulkan／CUDA 走 --gpu-backend + 设备 id；CPU 版挂 -ng
        # （CPU 版核心没有 ggml-vulkan/cuda DLL，硬给 --gpu-backend 会启动失败）
        if self._gpu_backend:
            cmd += ["--gpu-backend", self._gpu_backend,
                    "-dev", str(self._device_id)]

#         （英文／中文一致）。修 whisper 英文行首逗号（", ask not…"）与标点前
#         空格（"country ."）；出厂默认 qwen3+FA 中文末字被甩成单独一行
#         （「開放時間：早上九點至下午五」+「點。」——粘连标点让分行器游标多吞
#         一字、切点整体错位）；whisper+FA 中文逐字乱切。被 -ml 1 拆开的数字
#         并回（3.5／1,250／$1,250）；whisper 加 -sow 按词切段，长词不再拆成
#         "incom preh ensible"（FA 路径一律加，legacy 仅限明确选择的空白分词
#         语言——中文挂 -sow 会整句并段）。
#   性能：crispasr -t 默认取逻辑核心数（上限 16，可由 settings.cpu_threads
#         覆盖），原先只用 4 线程。CPU 推理 230s 音频：8 线程笔记本
#         20.9s→16.2s；128 线程服务器 turbo 107s→33s（64 线程反退 70s）。
#   加速：Linux 硬件检测（nvidia-smi／lspci）；CUDA 推荐限定 Windows（安装包

sed -n '116,128p' crisp_engine.py
def _infer_backend(model_path: Path | str) -> str:
    """依模型文件名推断 crispasr 后端（--backend）。

    CrispASR 是多后端 runtime（whisper / qwen3 / parakeet …），同一支
    crispasr.exe 靠 `--backend` 切换模型架构。OpenAI Whisper GGML 属 whisper
    架构（默认，免标志）；Qwen3-ASR GGUF 文件名含 'qwen3' → 走 qwen3 后端，1.7B
    用 'qwen3-1.7b'。判断错误时 crispasr 会自报模型不符，不致默默产生垃圾。
    """
    name = Path(model_path).name.lower()
    if "qwen3" in name:
        return "qwen3-1.7b" if "1.7b" in name else "qwen3"
    return "whisper"

pytest.ini pytest.ini View all files Repository files navigation README Contributing MIT license More items CrispASR One C++ binary, 148 backends — 71 of them TTS engines — plus multilingual text translation, zero Python dependencies. CrispASR started as a fork of whisper.cpp and extends that base into a unified speech engine called crispasr , backed by full ggml C++ runtimes for major open-weights ASR and TTS architectures. One build, one binary, one consistent CLI — pick the backend at the command line or let CrispASR auto-detect it from your GGUF file. See Text-to-Speech for the TTS side. $ crispasr -m ggml-base.en.bin -f samples/jfk.wav # OpenAI Whisper $ crispasr -m parakeet-tdt-0.6b.gguf -f samples/jfk.wav # NVIDIA Parakeet $ crispasr -m canary-1b-v2.gguf -f samples/jfk.wav # NVIDIA Canary $ crispasr -m voxtral-mini-3b-2507.gguf -f samples/jfk.wav # Mistral Voxtral $ crispasr --backend qwen3 -m auto -f samples/jfk.wav # -m auto downloads $ crispasr --backend kokoro -m auto --tts " Hello world " --tts-output out.wav # TTS No Python. No PyTorch. No separate per-model binary. No pip install . Just one C++ binary and a GGUF file. Browser : All backends compile to WebAssembly (4.3 MB) via build-wasm.sh . Multithreaded, runs entirely client-side with COOP/COEP headers. Demo : HuggingFace Space — live transcription + TTS + language detection, auto-deployed from hf-space/ . Ecosystem Project What it does CrispASR This repo — C++ speech engine. 148 backends (71 TTS), CLI + HTTP server + C-ABI + Python/Rust/Dart/Go/Ruby/Java bindings. CrisperWeaver Cross-platform Flutter transcription app built on CrispASR. Desktop + mobile, model browser with download queue, mic capture, SRT/VTT/JSON export, diarization, batch processing. Fully offline. CrispEmbed Text-related engine via ggml — same philosophy as CrispASR but for embeddings, retrieval, OCR and OMR, Math and Music Notation. Numerous architectures (XLM-R, Qwen3-Embed, Gemma3, ModernBERT, ...), dense + sparse + ColBERT + reranking. PP-OCR, Tesseract, EasyOCR, InternVL2, etc. Python/Rust/Dart bindings. Susurrus Python ASR GUI with 9 backends (faster-whisper, mlx-whisper, voxtral, insanely-fast-whisper, ...). The Python counterpart to CrispASR's C++ approach. which prebuilt Linux tarball to download — the -vulkan build requires the Vulkan driver and does not fall back to CPU (the -cuda tarballs do since v0.8.30, the -hip tarball since #586) Quick start — ASR Troubleshooting — it printed the banner and stopped, reading the exit code, --no-gpu bisect, which Windows zip Text-to-Speech (TTS) — 52 engines: Kokoro, Qwen3-TTS, VibeVoice, dots.tts, Orpheus, Chatterbox, IndexTTS, Irodori, VoxCPM2, CosyVoice3, CSM, Dia, Zonos, Bark, Piper, MeloTTS, and more Streaming & live transcription Server mode (HTTP API) Concurrency, parallelism & scaling — how one transcription uses multiple cores, concurrent server requests ( --server-workers ), bulk offline transcription, replicas behind a load balancer CLI reference — flags, VAD, CTC alignment, output formats, auto-download, audio formats Environment variables — the CRISPASR_<BACKEND>_<FEATURE> convention, global knobs, and every per-backend variable Language bindings — Python / Rust / Dart / Go / Java / JavaScript / Ruby / mobile Benchmarking CrispASR — how to measure transcribe time (not cold start): server/in-process reps, proof-of-work rules, phase-timing env vars Architecture — layered layout, src/core/ primitives, regression discipline Contributing — adding a new backend — 5-file recipe, ground-truth diff workflow Regression matrix — tools/test-all-backends.py capability tiers EU AI Act — synthetic-audio marking (watermark + C2PA + spoken disclaimer), what counts as a voice clone, the speaker-biometrics boundary, why there is no emotion recognition, and what stays your duty as deployer Quantize models — crispasr-quantize for all backends GPU backend selection Debugging & profiling Credits Start here Everything below this section is a catalogue — 100+ backends, browse it when you need one. If you just want CrispASR working , this is the whole path. No repo clone, no Python, no model hunting. New to the project? docs/getting-started.md walks the same path step by step, with the expected output and the three usual first-run failures. 1. Get the binary Download one file from Releases and unzip it: Platform Download Notes Windows crispasr-windows-x86_64-cpu.zip Needs AVX2 (2013+ Intel / 2015+ AMD). Older CPU → …-cpu-legacy.zip Windows + NVIDIA crispasr-windows-x86_64-cuda.zip Self-contained; a CUDA Toolkit install is not required. CUDA 12.6 legacy build: …-cuda126.zip (Pascal/Volta and newer, through Hopper); CUDA-13-native build: …-cuda13.zip (Turing+) macOS, Apple Silicon crispasr-macos-arm64.tar.gz Metal GPU + Accelerate; crispasr-macos.tar.gz is the arm64 compatibility alias macOS, Intel crispasr-macos-x86_64.tar.gz CPU + Accelerate; requires AVX2/FMA/F16C (Haswell or newer). Older Intel Mac → crispasr-macos-x86_64-cpu-legacy.tar.gz Linux crispasr-linux-x86_64.tar.gz …-cuda.tar.gz / …-vulkan.tar.gz for GPU Prefer to build it yourself? See Install & build . The -vulkan build requires the Vulkan driver and does not fall back to CPU; the Linux -cuda and -hip tarballs do fall back. Check it runs — this should print a version banner and exit: crispasr --version # Windows: .\crispasr.exe --version CUDA builds also print cuda toolkit and cuda runtime ABI , so this command distinguishes the CUDA 12 and CUDA 13 packages without inspecting DLLs. Windows CUDA packages bundle the runtime they were built against. The CUDA 12.6 legacy CLI and library assets use the -cuda126 suffix; their split packages require cuda126-runtime.zip from the same release. Use the included SHA-256 manifest when reusing runtime DLLs. Do not mix CUDA 12.8 binaries with CUDA 12.6 DLLs. The standard -cuda build retains CUDA 12.8 and native Blackwell support. CUDA 12.8 also supports Pascal and Volta in CrispASR; PyTorch's wheel architecture selection is a separate policy ( NVIDIA architecture support ). 2. Make it speak -m auto downloads the model on first use (~135 MB here) and reuses it afterwards — nothing to find or install. It lands in ~/.cache/crispasr/ ( %USERPROFILE%\.cache\crispasr on Windows). crispasr --backend kokoro -m auto --tts " The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog. " --tts-output hello.wav # crispasr: TTS output written to 'hello.wav' (78000 samples @ 24000 Hz, 3.25 sec) Play hello.wav . That is the TTS half working. 3. Transcribe it back crispasr --backend parakeet -m auto -f hello.wav -l en # crispasr: transcribed 3.2s audio in 0.32s (10.1x realtime) # The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog. (~467 MB on first run. -l en skips language auto-detection, which would otherwise fetch a small extra model.) Both halves now work — swap in your own .wav and you are running. Where to go next You want to… Go to Clone a voice from a recording docs/tts.md — and read the consent rules first; cloning requires --i-have-rights Pick a better ASR model Which backend should I pick? Transcribe Japanese with Haas-ja Haas-ja usage and models Transcribe with word timestamps, SRT/VTT docs/cli.md Live mic / streaming docs/streaming.md Run it as an HTTP server docs/server.md See every backend this binary has crispasr --list-backends If nothing happened Add -v to any command for verbose progress, and --dry-run-resolve to print which model files it would open (and whether they're on disk) without loading anything. If a command printed its banner and then simply stopped — no error, no output file — that is a crash, not a refusal, and the exit code identifies it in one step. See docs/troubleshooting.md . Supported backends CrispASR ships 148 backends (see the generated feature matrix for the authoritative, auto-counted list) — the majority for transcription/translation, and 71 TTS engines for synthesis. It also ships audio-to-audio S2S backends, including Sidon restoration and the VoxCPM2 AudioVAE speech upscaler; see the feature matrix for the complete capability list. Pick at the CLI with --backend NAME , or omit it to let the binary auto-detect from the GGUF metadata. Jump to the TTS table for the synthesis side. Output language. -tl <lang> (or -l ) selects the language to speak; cosyvoice3-tts , qwen3-tts and moss-tts act on it natively. For cross-lingual cloning — an English reference clip speaking German, the subtitle-dubbing case — also pass -sl <lang> for the language the reference is spoken in, so cosyvoice3 drops the reference transcript instead of carrying its accent. Over HTTP: "language" + "source_lang" on POST /v1/audio/speech . See docs/tts.md . Translation Text-to-text translation, distinct from the audio-side --translate flag (which routes audio → English text on whisper / canary / etc.). Driven by --text "..." -sl <src> -tl <tgt> . " \ -sl en -tl de # uses wmt21-dense-24-wide-en-x ./build/bin/crispasr --backend m2m100-wmt21 \ -m models/wmt21-dense-24-wide-x-en-q4_k.gguf \ --text " Le président a dit qu'il ne serait pas présent. " \ -sl fr -tl en # uses wmt21-dense-24-wide-x-en # Opus-MT / MarianMT (one small model per direction: `-m auto` is de→en, # `-m opus-mt-en-de` the other way; ~84 MB, CC-BY-4.0) ./build/bin/crispasr --backend marian -m auto \ --text " Die Konferenz findet am 3. Oktober in Berlin statt. " \ -sl de -tl en --beam-size 1 # → The conference will take place on 3 October in Berlin. # MADLAD-400 3B (419 languages, bit-token-identical to Python SP) ./build/bin/crispasr --backend madlad -m auto \ --text " Hello world. " \ -sl en -tl ta For 2-stage pipelines (e.g., ASR → m2m100), use the dedicated --tr-sl / --tr-tl flags; they fall back to -sl / -tl when unset, so single-stage standalone usage is just -sl/-tl . Post-processing models Work with all backends. Also available standalone for encode/decode. Model Architecture Sample Rate Token Rate License HuggingFace MioCodec v2 WavLM encoder → FSQ(12800) → Transformer decoder + AdaLN-Zero + SnakeBeta upsampler + iSTFT 44.1 kHz 25 Hz (341 bps) MIT cstr/miocodec-v2-44k-GGUF SNAC 24 kHz 3-codebook RVQ + decoder blocks (stride 8/8/4/2) 24 kHz 3×12.5 Hz MIT cstr/snac-24khz-GGUF All runtimes share ggml-based inference. The speech-LLM backends ( qwen3 , voxtral , voxtral4b , granite , glm-asr , kyutai-stt ) inject audio encoder frames directly into an autoregressive language model's input embeddings, instead of using a dedicated CTC/transducer/seq2seq decoder. The fastconformer-ctc backend hosts the NeMo FastConformer-CTC standalone ASR family — stt_en_fastconformer_ctc_{large,xlarge,xxlarge} and the architecturally-identical parakeet-ctc-{0.6b,1.1b} (different training data + tokenizer, same encoder + head shape) — with greedy CTC decoding. Same C++ runtime as the canary-ctc aligner. Music & audio analysis Beyond speech, CrispASR runs several music/audio analysis tasks — each a small GGUF with the architecture auto-detected, no Python. See docs/cli.md for the per-task flags and output formats. Source separation ( --separate ) — split a mix into stems ( <input>_<stem>.wav ) via mel-band-roformer (vocal/instrumental, MIT), htdemucs (4-stem), or mini-bs-roformer (HiDolen V2.5, 46.2M parameters, stereo bass/drums/other/vocals at 44.1 kHz). --stems vocals,drums selects a subset; --sep-output-dir sets the output location. Mini BS RoFormer uses an explicitly converted local GGUF. The SCNet / SCNet-large candidate backend adds native four-stem separation with local F16/Q8_0/Q4_K GGUFs; original-checkpoint fidelity and paired performance remain qualification gates. Voice isolation ( --separate with a voice-isolator GGUF) — Inworld Voice Isolator v1 (Apache-2.0, 48 kHz): cleans noisy speech into <input>_voice.wav plus the removed <input>_background.wav . Offline and realtime (causal, 20 ms look-ahead) checkpoints; also served by --separate-model on /v1/audio/separation and Session.separate() . Piano transcription ( --backend piano-transcription ) — piano audio → note events (88 keys @ 100 fps, ByteDance/Kong CRNN; F16 GGUF ≈ 77 MB). Polyphonic note events ( --backend basic-pitch ) — Spotify Basic Pitch, any instrument → note events (~110 KB model). Multi-instrument transcription ( --backend mt3 , alias music-transcription ) — MT3's T5 encoder/decoder emits note events with per-instrument programs (F16 GGUF ≈ 96 MB). Onsets & Frames ( --backend onsets-and-frames ) — Hawthorne et al.'s piano transcriber (MIT), the best solo-piano model here per megabyte: 69.0% note F1 on MusicNet's piano pieces against Basic Pitch's 57.5% , and F1-identical to its ONNX export at f32 and Q8_0. Q4_0 GGUF 18.6 MiB, Q8_0 30.8 MiB, F32 101.9 MiB. See docs/music-transcription/ONSETS_AND_FRAMES.md . hFT-Transformer ( --backend hft-transformer ) — Toyama et al.'s hierarchical frequency-time transformer (MIT), the most accurate solo-piano model here and by far the smallest: 70.5% note F1 on MusicNet's piano pieces from 5.5 M parameters, Q8_0 GGUF 7.0 MiB, Q4_0 4.5 MiB, F32 21.8 MiB. It is also much the most expensive to run — 249 GFLOP of matrix multiply per 2 s of audio, and quantisation makes it slower , not faster, on a CPU without int8 dot-product instructions. Read docs/music-transcription/HFT_TRANSFORMER.md before choosing it over onsets-and-frames . All five take --piano-format text|json|midi ; midi writes a Standard MIDI File. Guitar tablature ( --tab ) — per-frame fret-per-string grid via TabCNN (Wiggins & Kim, ISMIR 2019; CC BY 4.0 weights). The backend emits per-string emission scores, not a decided tablature — run your own constrained Viterbi via crispasr_session_tab_emissions() for playable output. Beat / downbeat tracking ( --beats ) — beat grid via Beat This! (CPJKU, ISMIR 2024; MIT for code and weights, no patent-encumbered DBN). Chord recognition ( --chords ) — chord timeline ( .lab ) via BTC (ISMIR 2019). Weights are CC-BY-NC-SA, gated behind --accept-license cc-by-nc-sa-4.0 . Pitch / F0 estimation ( --pitch ) — monophonic pitch track via CREPE (MIT). Feature matrix Run crispasr --list-backends to see it live. Each backend declares capabilities at runtime; if you ask for a feature the selected backend does not support, CrispASR prints a warning and silently ignores the flag. Sortable / filterable view: docs/feature-matrix.html — click any column header to sort, type to filter rows, click cap pills to require a capability. Generated from crispasr --list-backends-json (single source of truth — drift impossible). Regenerate via python tools/gen-feature-matrix.py . A Markdown twin lives at docs/feature-matrix.md . The static table below is a curated subset focusing on the ASR backends and the cross-cutting features that matter for ASR pipelines. The full 119-backend × 27-cap surface is in the generated views. See docs/feature-matrix.md for the full 109-backend matrix. TTS-only backends ( kokoro , qwen3-tts + variants, vibevoice-tts , orpheus + DE variants, chatterbox / chatterbox-turbo / chatterbox-nano / kartoffelbox-turbo / lahgtna-chatterbox , dia , bark , outetts , zonos , csm , f5-tts , irodori-tts , supertonic , parler-tts , speecht5 , piper , fastpitch , pocket-tts , melotts , cosyvoice3 , voxcpm2 , tada-tts ) all carry the TTS, AUTO_DOWNLOAD, TEMPERATURE, and FLASH_ATTN caps; per-backend cloning + voice-pack support is documented in the Text-to-Speech models table above and docs/tts.md . The vibevoice and lfm2-audio columns mark dual-mode (ASR + TTS) backends. Nemotron managed downloads use pinned F16 (~1.3 GB); the former Q4 default fails the fixed CPU/T4 original-reference controls. See the default qualification and existing-release usage . Key: ✔ = native/built-in, -am = via CTC forced aligner ( -am canary-ctc-aligner.gguf or -am qwen3-forced-aligner.gguf ), LID = via external language identification pre-step ( -l auto ), pp = via --punc-model post-processor (FireRedPunc or fullstop-punc), * = experimental or partial support, † = PLUS variant only (native [T:N] word timestamps with -owts ; base uses -am ). granite-4.1 covers both the regular and -plus variants; granite-4.1-nar is a non-autoregressive variant with encoder+projector only (no LLM decode features). The KV quant row marks backends that honor CRISPASR_KV_QUANT={f16,q8_0,q4_0} — CTC-style backends without a KV cache (parakeet, fc-ctc, wav2vec2, kyutai-stt, firered, moonshine variants, omniasr-CTC) don't apply. The same backends also honor the per-half CRISPASR_KV_QUANT_K / CRISPASR_KV_QUANT_V overrides (llama.cpp --cache-type-k / --cache-type-v parity) for asymmetric K-vs-V precision; common recipe K=q8_0 V=q4_0 saves ~40 % more KV memory than symmetric Q8_0. The mmap weights row marks backends consuming core_gguf::load_weights() and therefore honoring CRISPASR_GGUF_MMAP=1 ; whisper itself uses upstream's loader and is unaffected. See docs/cli.md Memory footprint for usage + recommended combos. Speaker diarization as a post-processing step via --diarize : energy / xcorr — stereo-only, no extra deps foxnose — best accuracy, no external deps : WeSpeaker ResNet34-LM embeddings + GMM/BIC speaker counting + spectral clustering + Viterbi smoothing. Estimates the speaker count rather than needing it up front; --diarize-embedder auto fetches the GGUF (24 MB, CC-BY-4.0). 7.3 % DER on VoxConverse dev where pyannote + TitaNet scores 7.8 %, and 3.18 % vs the upstream reference's 3.07 % when scored on turns ( more ) sortformer — NVIDIA Nemotron-3-Diarization (streaming Sortformer v3, #466): one end-to-end network, up to 8 speakers in order of appearance, overlap-aware, no embedder or clustering. --diarize-model auto fetches NVIDIA's 107 MB q8_0 GGUF (OpenMDW-1.1, commercial use allowed). Matches transformers exactly on every p > 0.5 decision ( more ) pyannote — native GGUF (no Python, no sherpa-onnx); add --diarize-embedder auto (TitaNet) or --diarize-embedder indextts (ECAPA-TDNN) for globally stable speaker IDs across long files sherpa / ecapa — external sherpa-onnx subprocess; runs once globally on full audio for consistent speaker IDs (#110) vad-turns — mono-friendly gap-based proxy The server endpoint supports response_format=diarized_json for structured speaker-labelled output with normalised speaker letters (A, B, C …) — see docs/server.md . Full reference + tuning knobs (cluster threshold, max speakers, pluggable embedder adapters): see docs/cli.md#diarization . Language identification for backends without native LID: --lid-backend whisper (default, 75 MB ggml-tiny.bin), --lid-backend silero (native GGUF, 16 MB, 95 languages), or --lid-backend firered (FireRedLID, 1.7 GB, 120 languages — Conformer encoder + Transformer decoder). Voice activity detection : --vad uses the default Silero VAD (~885 KB, auto-downloaded). Each VAD segment is transcribed independently, producing separate SRT/VTT entries with correct timestamps. # CrispASR

**One C++ binary, 148 backends — 71 of them TTS engines — plus multilingual text translation, zero Python dependencies.**

CrispASR started as a fork of [whisper.cpp](https://github.com/ggml-org/whisper.cpp) and extends that base into a **unified speech engine** called `crispasr`, backed by full ggml C++ runtimes for major open-weights ASR *and* TTS architectures. One build, one binary, one consistent CLI — pick the backend at the command line or let CrispASR auto-detect it from your GGUF file. See [Text-to-Speech](#text-to-speech-models) for the TTS side.

```console
$ crispasr -m ggml-base.en.bin          -f samples/jfk.wav                    # OpenAI Whisper
$ crispasr -m parakeet-tdt-0.6b.gguf    -f samples/jfk.wav                    # NVIDIA Parakeet
$ crispasr -m canary-1b-v2.gguf         -f samples/jfk.wav                    # NVIDIA Canary
$ crispasr -m voxtral-mini-3b-2507.gguf -f samples/jfk.wav                    # Mistral Voxtral
$ crispasr --backend qwen3 -m auto      -f samples/jfk.wav                    # -m auto downloads
$ crispasr --backend kokoro -m auto --tts "Hello world" --tts-output out.wav  # TTS
```

No Python. No PyTorch. No separate per-model binary. No `pip install`. Just one C++ binary and a GGUF file.

**Browser**: All backends compile to WebAssembly (4.3 MB) via `build-wasm.sh`.
Multithreaded, runs entirely client-side with COOP/COEP headers.

**Demo**: [HuggingFace Space](https://huggingface.co/spaces/cstr/CrispASR) —
live transcription + TTS + language detection, auto-deployed from `hf-space/`.

### Ecosystem

| Project | What it does |
|---|---|
| **[CrispASR](https://github.com/CrispStrobe/CrispASR)** | This repo — C++ speech engine. 148 backends (71 TTS), CLI + HTTP server + C-ABI + Python/Rust/Dart/Go/Ruby/Java bindings. |
| **[CrisperWeaver](https://github.com/CrispStrobe/CrisperWeaver)** | Cross-platform Flutter transcription app built on CrispASR. Desktop + mobile,  model browser with download queue, mic capture, SRT/VTT/JSON export, diarization, batch processing. Fully offline. |
| **[CrispEmbed](https://github.com/CrispStrobe/CrispEmbed)** | Text-related engine via ggml — same philosophy as CrispASR but for embeddings, retrieval, OCR and OMR, Math and Music Notation. Numerous architectures (XLM-R, Qwen3-Embed, Gemma3, ModernBERT, ...), dense + sparse + ColBERT + reranking. PP-OCR, Tesseract, EasyOCR, InternVL2, etc. Python/Rust/Dart bindings. |
| **[Susurrus](https://github.com/CrispStrobe/Susurrus)** | Python ASR GUI with 9 backends (faster-whisper, mlx-whisper, voxtral, insanely-fast-whisper, ...). The Python counterpart to CrispASR's C++ approach. |
